### Cache and embedded session-store TTL lifecycle boundary — 2026-10-18

The remaining state-lifetime audit found unchecked i64 millisecond-to-Duration multiplications in direct cache writes, cache protocol SET/CAS and embedded session-store SET/CAS. Oversized positive TTLs could wrap to no expiry, while an extreme negative CAS TTL could wrap to a 1 ms lifetime. All five sites now use `state_store.ttl_from_milliseconds`: nonpositive values consistently mean no expiry, and positive values above 9223372036854 ms return `state_store_ttl_out_of_range` before any mutation. Existing values survive rejected writes; direct cache error counters remain accurate. Delete operations still ignore TTL. No new pruning worker or clock-policy change.

Original controls reproduce three cache failures (one valid-boundary pass) and two actual QuickJS host-callback failures. The fix passes 21 related tests: six generic store/conversion tests, thirteen cache TTL/listener-lifecycle tests, and two embedded host-callback tests. Positive SET/CAS expiry, maximum valid TTL, preservation after rejection, no-expiry negatives and delete semantics are covered. Cache Unix binds initially hit sandbox EPERM; the identical compiled binary passes with approved local socket access, with the environmental failure retained. Safety lint, new-source/test formatting and diff checks pass. A DB-enabled V3/QuickJS/OpenSSL/Boehm candidate builds from 458 frozen production V inputs and passes CLI help. ### Deterministic metrics load admission — 2026-10-18

The preceding metrics E2E timeout is resolved as a fixture admission assumption. A simultaneous burst of short HTTP handlers does not guarantee executor concurrency: the inspected macOS fasthttp reactor accepts up to 32 sockets in a batch and executes their handlers synchronously on the accepting worker. The candidate's earlier wait for observable queue occupancy and the old binary's wait for timeout counter growth therefore had no guaranteed trigger.

### Feishu tenant-token credential lifetime ownership — 2026-10-19

Provider-instance credential replacement previously left the old tenant-token cache eligible for reuse. A refresh already in HTTP flight could also cache and return its old-credential result after rotation. FeishuState now owns a private identity for each app's current credentials: configuration publication and token invalidation happen together under its mutex, while counters, connection observations and events survive. Token admission captures resolved app, credentials, identity and cache eligibility under that same mutex; HTTP runs outside it. Only a current identity can publish a refresh result, and the caller returns a credential-change error for a rejected response. A→B→A cannot revive old authority; unchanged credentials retain valid cache and refresh authority. Empty app resolution now uses the resolved app's cache. ### WebSocket executor mailbox selection ownership — 2026-10-19

Serialized WebSocket mailboxes used a boolean running marker per key. After affinity withdrawal and key reuse, old completion/cancellation/restore could clear a replacement's marker; cancellation before lane transfer could instead strand its own marker. A retired selection could also overwrite a new lane binding. The running map now records the selected task control identity, and terminal paths release only a matching claim. Pending cancellation releases its own claim independently of its lane lease. Binding, worker enqueue and execution start verify the claim, covering withdrawal before deferred cancellation reaches an old task. Stale restore cancels/notifies the old control outside the executor lock. Existing native close/lane release owners and shutdown notification remain intact; the unused key-only finish helper is removed. Sources: [mailbox selection and restore](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_websocket_mailbox.v), [task lifecycle](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_task_control.v), [worker admission](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_task_admission.v) and [ownership regressions](../src/executor/mailbox_claim_owner_test.v).

An initial cleanup-only fix still admits retired work into the worker queue; a later enqueue guard still permits already queued retired work to begin before deferred cancellation. Both publication windows are closed in the final implementation. ### WebSocket affinity connection binding and terminal ownership — 2026-10-19

The mailbox lane binder recorded connection/key maps without adding connection references, so last close could leave pending work without withdrawal/cancellation. Initial binding now adds one reference atomically; repeated binding is idempotent. A queued pre-migration frame also cannot implicitly move a connection back to a different old key. Explicit migration now joins an existing target key's canonical lane rather than displacing it with the source lane; a fresh target still inherits the source/current lane. Sources: [binding owner](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_websocket_mailbox.v), [reference release and migration](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_websocket_affinity_state.v) and [ownership regressions](../src/executor/affinity_binding_owner_test.v).

A denied open returns before a native WebSocket session starts, so it cannot rely on a future close callback ([ingress denial](../src/websocket_ingress_runtime.v)). The [response finalizer](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_websocket_event_runtime.v) now releases affinity/actor state for denied opens and close events before response-supplied migration or cache publication. This prevents retained denied-open registrations and phantom persistent keys created from close replies. Accepted-open actor caching and the existing process-lifetime sticky key→lane policy remain intact; this slice does not add eviction/TTL to that lane map.

### OpenAI mapped stream terminal and fallback ownership — 2026-10-19

A completed NDJSON row could finish the downstream HTTP stream while the [mapped progress callback](../src/openai_mapped_stream_proxy_runtime.v) continued mapping later rows in the same network chunk. Fallback now receives a fresh chunk decoder instead of inheriting the primary request's plain/chunked/completed state.

### OpenAI executor stream terminal and generator ownership — 2026-10-19

The executor Chat and Responses batch paths continued processing mappings after completion. Chat could write beyond its final HTTP chunk; Responses could emit later deltas and duplicate completion events. The [Chat output owner](../src/openai_executor_stream_runtime.v) now shares terminal-aware mapping output between callbacks and batch results, claims terminal/error rows before I/O, and stops batch iteration at completion or write failure. The [Responses owner](../src/openai_responses_executor_runtime.v) separates wire termination from optional completed-body caching: `response.completed`, `response.failed` and `response.incomplete` write their terminal event then cancel the generator through the existing callback/iterator-return path. ### OpenAI passthrough fallback response ownership — 2026-10-19

The primary request normalized the private `[DONE]` fetch abort to success, while the fallback helper reported it as an error. After a primary HTTP error, a successful fallback SSE response could therefore be followed by a second HTTP response. True fallback fetch failures could also fall through with reset status 200 and stale primary error information. The [passthrough owner](../src/openai_stream_proxy_runtime.v) now uses the same completion-aware fetch helper for primary/fallback requests and handles fallback errors before the old response-selection fallthrough: failures before output return 502 with their own error; failures after commit only finalize/close the existing response. Empty progress calls no longer prematurely commit headers, and short plain error bodies buffered by chunk auto-detection are retained at EOF. Done-state late progress cannot mutate the response.

Validation: four of five [new regressions](../src/openai_passthrough_fallback_owner_test.v) fail on the original code; the natural-EOF control remains passing. Real upstream TCP fixtures, a planner plugin and raw downstream bytes verify one HTTP response, plain/chunked completion, malformed-upstream 502 behavior and backup HTTP status/error retention. Six suites pass 69 functions, including 28 real TCP OpenAI gateway integration cases, HTTP socket ownership and prior mapped/executor terminal regressions. Safety lint, touched formatting, diff checks and the independent DB-enabled candidate build pass. Complete standard E2E passes on the first attempt (CLI 4/config-runtime 205 and all standard fixtures). Evidence: `tmp/openai-passthrough-fallback-owner-validation/report.txt`; tested hashes match and the root binary/SDK fixes are preserved.

### OpenAI passthrough SSE terminal-event ownership — 2026-10-19

The [passthrough stream owner](../src/openai_stream_proxy_runtime.v) previously recognized `[DONE]` by substring after forwarding the entire progress callback. Content/comment/other-field text could therefore end a live stream; incomplete records could terminate early; a valid no-space data field was missed; and payload after a terminal event in the same callback escaped into the response. Completion now requires an exact single-data-field terminal event and its blank-line boundary. A bounded line-prefix recognizer handles fragmented LF/CRLF/CR, optional field space and the initial UTF-8 BOM without accumulating event bodies. It claims completion before output, publishes only through the terminal boundary, and resets with each fallback attempt. The existing fetch-abort owner releases the upstream connection at that boundary.

Validation: four failure classes reproduce on the original implementation in `baseline.log`; all seven [new regressions](../src/openai_passthrough_terminal_owner_test.v) pass on the final source. They cover false markers, incomplete events, same-callback trailing bytes, fragmented endings/BOM, late callbacks, fallback reset, and real plain/chunked upstream sockets kept open after the terminal event. Six related suites pass 68 functions, including all 28 gateway integration cases and prior fallback/mapped/executor ownership regressions. Safety lint, touched formatting, diff checks and an independent DB-enabled build pass. Complete standard E2E passes on the first attempt (CLI 4/config-runtime 205 and all standard fixtures). Evidence: `tmp/openai-passthrough-terminal-owner-validation/report.txt`; final input hashes match the candidate source and the root binary is unchanged.

### Codex released-stream pending RPC routing ownership — 2026-10-19

Stream release already cleared message targets, active/thread routes and read fallback, but pending RPCs still supplied fallback routing and send admission. The [response owner](../src/codex_rpc_response_runtime.v) consumes a retired record and returns before thread capture, error aggregation or business dispatch. ### Relay carrier target and pending-message lifetime ownership — 2026-10-20

A [Relay carrier](../src/ws/relay_carrier.v) previously retained only a public connection ID and resolved the current WebSocket on every operation. A stale carrier could observe, send/cancel or close a replacement, or append to a future-open queue. [Pipeline reply dispatch](../src/relay_pipeline_runtime.v) also created the carrier after business callbacks, allowing a callback to replace the target before capture. The required context now binds one target at carrier construction: it captures lifecycle or absence under the hub mutex, and its connected/send/close callbacks retain that authority. The [App context factory](../src/websocket_runtime.v) provides this bound target; pipeline ingress creates it before business dispatch. Missing or retired carriers cannot acquire a later registration.

The [owned send boundary](../src/ws/hub_runtime.v) checks the captured lifecycle while selecting the native handle and accepting queued data. Native completion continues on the captured handle without another ID lookup. [Pending messages](../src/ws/types.v) accepted by a known opening registration now retain its lifecycle; [queue claims and flush](../src/ws/runtime.v) discard retired-registration messages and retain the claimed writer. Borrowed native clients cannot append into a different current client's opening queue. Explicitly unregistered future-open messages and current-lifecycle messages keep their existing behavior.

### Initial Feishu HTTP send completion publication — 2026-10-20

The [command handler](../src/command_handlers.v) and [bridge proxy server](../src/feishu_card_bridge_server_runtime.v) registered initial send results after HTTP without retaining publication admission. Clear before the first buffer existed could be undone by completion; binding during HTTP could be overwritten, and an old completion could join a fresh same-stream send. Both entry points now capture an opaque [InitialSend claim](../src/feishu/initial_send.v) from the original FeishuState before sending. Explicit buffer registration revokes affected stream scopes; stream clear revokes even with no existing buffer; message/chain release records the actual withdrawn IDs. Command clear reports successful pending-send revocation. Completion checks the state owner, exact scope, one-shot registration, existing result-ID ownership and changes to that ID since admission. The command pairs buffer/target publication under Feishu → Codex locking; HTTP and sent-notification callbacks run outside that boundary. Rejected completion reports feishu_initial_send_retired and suppresses the sent notification while retaining the physical message ID for diagnostics.

The [state owner](../src/feishu/types.v) retains message-ID mutation history only while initial sends remain outstanding. This rejects a result ID rebound and then deleted during HTTP. Final completion/error releases its own registration once, preserves peers and newer scopes, and clears temporary history when the last send exits. Distinct current sends can both publish. Cross-state claims cannot commit or decrement the original owner's accounting. Already-issued HTTP operations finish through their original transport owner.

The original eight [integration regressions](../src/feishu_initial_send_owner_test.v) produce six assertion failures on saved production originals; the parallel-success and failure/retry controls pass. Failure evidence includes the real HTTP adapter recreating om_test_1 after clear, plus gated command/bridge completion, rebind, result-ID collision and fresh-generation interleaving. Final validation passes 95 functions from nine source files: ten initial-send integration functions, seven [claim/state functions](../src/feishu/initial_send_test.v), and 78 existing command/bridge/binding/release/commit/rollover/finish functions. Added final cases cover rebind-then-delete and concurrent completion/clear. Two legacy [bridge fixtures](../src/command_provider_host_test.v) now establish their sender registrations and pass the current client ID argument to pending registration; existing assertions remain intact. Temporary combined testing normalizes the executor import alias only for the source using its bare import. Initial fixture compile errors and an aborted incorrect alias conversion are archived separately from negative regression evidence. The final combined entry was removed after passing.

The isolated DB-enabled OpenSSL/Boehm candidate passes complete standard E2E: CLI 4/config-runtime 205, all standard Node/PHP/protocol checks and eight cold-hub concurrent Relay cases. Safety lint, eight-file format verification and diff checks pass. All 467 production/support staging inputs match the final workspace, and test/candidate/root-binary hash checks pass. The root binary is unchanged; no SDK or E2E thresholds changed. Evidence: `tmp/feishu-initial-send-owner-validation/report.txt`, `incremental.diff`, saved originals, negative baseline and separate final logs. Live DB service and installed WordPress scenarios remain opt-in.

This slice governs local initial-result publication after observed bind/clear operations. Wider command admission, cross-host cancellation propagation, whole-project V suites and prolonged load remain separate audit work.

### Feishu stream-command admission and failure completion — 2026-10-20

The [command handler](../src/command_handlers.v) and [bridge server](../src/feishu_card_bridge_server_runtime.v) previously operated append/finish/fail through message-ID lookups. Append could recreate withdrawn buffers, finish could seal a replacement, and fail could delete it. Missing targets could fall through to ordinary update; a legacy event could also bypass canonical stream dispatch. Stream commands now obtain an opaque [StreamCommand admission](../src/feishu/stream_command.v) against the local state owner, matching stream and optional app before retaining the exact buffer identity. Bound buffers require the matching stream ID. Missing, mismatched and retired contexts return feishu_stream_command_retired. Canonical stream kinds stay on this path. [Command ports](../src/command_host.v) and [bridge ports](../src/feishu_card_bridge_host.v) require the admission-bearing operation, with test hosts adapted without changing their assertions.

The [runtime adapter](../src/feishu_stream_command_runtime.v) revalidates the same admission. Append validates and mutates under the buffer mutex, then performs any rollover I/O outside it. Rollover links retain the exact successor identity, preventing traversal into a recreated same-stream/message successor. Finish uses a captured snapshot through the existing identity-bound preview/finish claims. Failure installs an exclusive in-flight marker, cancels earlier rollover/finish publishers and closes command/preview admission before HTTP. Its original operation owns final deletion on success or error; a rebound buffer rejects old cleanup. That cleanup preserves initial-send scopes admitted after failure began. Bridge clients recheck local identity around RPC, forward the captured app when omitted, preserve stream correlation and distinguish preview flush from terminal finish. The server captures its own local admission before invoking the runtime.

The original twelve [App regressions](../src/feishu_stream_admission_test.v) produce eleven assertion failures against saved production originals; the current-command control passes. Final validation passes 121 functions from eleven sources: nineteen new App/command/bridge functions, seven new [state functions](../src/feishu/stream_command_test.v), and 95 existing initial-send/command/bridge/binding/release/commit/rollover/finish functions. Coverage includes same-stream rebind/recreation after admission, missing targets/buffers, old stream/app context, canonical-kind precedence, bridge checks before and after RPC, forwarded app/preview context, a real HTTP fail completion across rebind, foreign-owner rejection, successor identity and fresh initial-send preservation. Two new fail fixtures initially lacked valid interactive-card request data and were corrected without changing production validation. That diagnostic log is separate from the original production-negative baseline. A pre-app-context candidate passes its 107 integration functions; the final app-forwarding candidate independently passes all 107 again, plus the unchanged fourteen state functions. The final temporary combined test entry was removed.

The final isolated DB-enabled OpenSSL/Boehm candidate passes complete standard E2E: CLI 4/config-runtime 205, all standard Node/PHP/protocol fixtures and eight cold-hub concurrent Relay cases. Safety lint, fifteen-file formatting and diff checks pass. All 469 production/support inputs match final staging and workspace; final source/test/candidate hashes verify, and the root binary is unchanged. No SDK or E2E thresholds changed. Evidence: `tmp/feishu-stream-admission-validation/report.txt`, `incremental.diff`, saved originals, negative baseline, separate fixture diagnostics and final logs. Live DB service and installed WordPress scenarios remain opt-in.

Linux stopped after read-only preflight on pre-existing tracked changes under the VM skill's explicit rule; its compiler remains clean local-dev (V 0.5.2, 4f3082f), with no Linux build/test or dependency mutation. This slice protects local admitted operations and rejects distinguishable stale stream/app contexts. Existing wire fields carry no buffer generation tag: an older packet first arriving after identical stream/app/message identifiers have already been reused cannot prove its old origin with those identifiers alone. Wire generation/cancellation propagation, wider ingress admission, whole-project V suites and prolonged load remain separate audit work. Already-admitted HTTP operations retain their original transport owner and may finish; their local cleanup/publication cannot transfer into a replacement buffer.

### Feishu stream-command wire generation — 2026-10-20

The previous admission guard protected work already captured locally, but could not distinguish an old packet first arriving after identical message/stream/app IDs were reused. [Wire admission](../src/feishu/stream_command.v) now requires nonempty `metadata.stream_generation` equal to the current buffer generation before granting the existing local identity-bound admission. Both the [command handler](../src/command_handlers.v) and [bridge server](../src/feishu_card_bridge_server_runtime.v) enforce this for append, finish and fail. Missing/mismatched tags return `feishu_stream_command_retired` without HTTP or registry mutation; ingress never fills a missing tag from current state.

Each explicit buffer registration, including identical-ID session.bind, creates a fresh server generation. Bind activity and the `feishu.message.sent` event return the generation captured under the publication lock; callbacks run after unlock and do not re-read a replacement. Initial sends may supply a fresh producer-allocated generation to support send-plus-append in one response batch; producers must never recycle it for a new stream lifecycle. Otherwise the server allocates the tag and returns it in the sent event. [Initial-send publication](../src/feishu/initial_send.v) installs the producer/remote tag only after the existing completion claim commits. Bridge send results carry the remote generation into the local mirror. Rollover successors preserve the logical stream generation while retaining separate local heap identities. Cancellation remains checked independently on each host: a matching client mirror cannot grant authority over a rebound remote buffer.

The [TypeScript command builders](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/commands.mts) now accept a captured stream handle containing streamId and streamGeneration. The [SQLite stream store](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/state.mjs) allocates and persists the tag for each newly created/derived stream; parent, item, approval and recovery paths pass that handle when constructing sends/appends/finishes. An ID-only builder emits no authority. The PHP example persists tags from sent receipts, preserves them across update-only receipts, selects them with task context, and includes them in append/finish metadata; fresh and existing DB schemas gain the column. This is a strict protocol migration: older stream-command producers must propagate the tag, and already-persisted handles with no tag cannot recover permission by ID lookup. Fresh producer lifecycles or explicit binding receipts establish new authority. The new [serialized-command regressions](../tests/protocol/feishu-stream-generation.test.mjs) are part of standard E2E; [PHP receipt/migration regression](../tests/protocol/feishu-generation-receipt.php) runs independently of the legacy example SDK.

Final validation passes 207 distinct related V test functions: 111 command/App/bridge/binding/release/commit/rollover/finish functions, 17 state functions and all 79 TypeScript example functions from seven source suites. New regressions cover packets serialized before identical-ID rebind/recreation, missing/foreign tags, stale bridge frames, bind receipt rotation, initial-send generation publication and remote/mirror matching; the existing producer callback regression additionally checks matching nonempty send/append tags. Two Node serialized-command functions pass, and the independent PHP test passes actual receipt routing, task-context selection, persistence, update-only preservation and old-DB migration. The initial integration run exposed one remaining old-format test fixture; it was updated without weakening assertions. The initial combined producer compile exposed duplicate names in separate test suites; final generated names are source-qualified with all bodies retained. A final fixture vfmt change is layout only and archived separately. No production source changed during/after validation.

The isolated DB-enabled OpenSSL/Boehm build passes complete standard E2E, including the new Node protocol tests, CLI 4/config-runtime 205, all standard Node/PHP/admin/provider/Relay/MCP/stream/metrics fixtures and all eight cold-hub concurrent first-Relay cases. E2E started after all compilations finished and ran alongside the final producer-test runtime. All 469 staged production/support inputs match the workspace; source/candidate hashes, formatting, safety lint and diff checks pass. The root binary is unchanged, all three temporary combined test entries were removed, and evidence is recorded in `tmp/feishu-wire-generation-validation/report.txt`, saved originals, incremental diff, combined sources/manifests and final logs. No E2E thresholds or dependency versions changed. Live DB services and installed WordPress scenarios remain opt-in.

The complete legacy PHP example Pest suite could not bootstrap because its `VHttpd\Upstream\WebSocket\EventHandler` SDK interface is absent from this checkout; its failure is recorded separately from the passing standalone PHP protocol test. Linux validation stopped after read-only preflight found pre-existing tracked .gitignore/.qoder changes under the explicit VM skill rule; Linux V remains clean local-dev, V 0.5.2 4f3082f, with no Linux build/test or dependency mutation. Broader control-command replay admission, cross-host cancellation delivery, upstream notification provenance, whole-project V suites and prolonged load remain separate work. This generation tag protects serialized append/finish/fail authority and its publication; it is not a replacement for those other admission/cancellation boundaries.

### Feishu control-command admission and target provenance — 2026-10-20

The [control transition](../src/feishu/control_generation.v) now guards existing ownership before the [command handler](../src/command_handlers.v) mutates it. Replacing a bound message with session.bind requires its captured `metadata.stream_generation`; a stale nonempty expectation cannot recreate a withdrawn buffer. Session.clear requires a nonempty tag and validates target, stream and optional app before touching pending sends or either registry. Message-level clear releases its owned physical chain while preserving independent initial sends; stream-level clear selects only its generation/app and revokes only the matching pending scope. Recreated successors are protected by physical link identity as well as generation/app. The trusted legacy chain-release helper likewise validates the root before revoking a scope and stops at foreign successor ownership.

[Initial-send scopes](../src/feishu/initial_send.v) capture producer generation and app at admission. A request conflicting with live buffers or pending ownership is rejected before HTTP; the [bridge server](../src/feishu_card_bridge_server_runtime.v) independently checks its own state. Existing pointer-bound completion/retirement still protects admitted HTTP results. First sends can use the existing server-assigned generation, but cancellation before the first receipt requires a preallocated producer generation. Ingress does not recover authority by looking up a newer tag. The [TypeScript clear builder](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/commands.mts) serializes the captured handle, and both [cancellation paths](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/thread-task-commands.mjs) pass that handle; actual detached/interrupt regressions assert the tag is present.

Narrowing clear to owned targets requires provenance for followup cards that have no independent buffer. [CodexTarget](../src/codex/types.v) now records `stream_generation` and `buffer_message_id`: initial/bind targets own themselves, rollover targets own their child buffer, and [finish targets](../src/feishu_stream_buffer_runtime.v) retain their root buffer owner. An admitted initial result replaces stale same-message index entries instead of preserving an old generation through deduplication. The [paired release](../src/codex_provider_runtime_state.v) matches selected owner/message IDs and generation, removes only Feishu targets, and retains other platforms, generations and message owners. Untagged legacy targets are removable only by explicitly released physical IDs. Main and every stored instance entry are mutated directly, including legacy main/default aliases, so normalized lookup cannot overwrite a stored alias with the main runtime. Remaining alias targets preserve their route/error owner; last-owner cleanup also cancels error-only jobs with no materialized runtime instance. Selection and publication keep the FeishuState -> CodexState lock order, with no HTTP/notification callback inside it.

Final validation passes 155 distinct related V functions: the [129-function integration combination](../src/feishu_control_generation_test.v) covers control/App/bridge/initial/bind/release/commit/rollover/finish/Codex-route behavior and three selected actual TypeScript cancellation paths; [26 state functions](../src/feishu/control_generation_test.v) include nine new control/chain functions plus seventeen existing admission/initial-send/wire-generation functions. Three [Node protocol functions](../tests/protocol/feishu-stream-generation.test.mjs) pass. An initial broad fixture migration introduced invalid test fields and was repaired; an intermediate runtime run exposed three old-format clear fixtures. Their captured context was updated while retaining their intended success/paired-publication assertions. An intermediate 128-function run passed before the final stale-initial-target metadata refresh; the final 129-function input independently passed. Temporary combined inputs and manifests retain the actual assertions and verified hashes, and all temporary source entries were removed. No original-production-negative baseline is claimed for those fixture diagnostics.

The final isolated DB-enabled OpenSSL/Boehm build passes complete standard E2E: CLI 4/config-runtime 205, the three Node protocol functions, all standard Node/PHP/admin/provider/Relay/MCP/stream/metrics fixtures and eight cold-hub concurrent first-Relay cases. Its separate staging directory contains 470 production/support files matching the workspace; final input hashes, formatting, safety lint and diff checks pass, and the root binary is unchanged. E2E ran after the final compilation and regression completed. Evidence: `tmp/feishu-control-generation-validation/report.txt`, saved originals, incremental diff, source/manifests, separately named intermediate logs and final logs. No dependency versions or E2E thresholds changed. Live DB services and installed WordPress remain opt-in; whole-project V suites are not claimed.

Its compiler remains clean local-dev, V 0.5.2 4f3082f, with no Linux build/test/install/mutation. This slice protects existing buffer owners, pending-send admission and paired local target cleanup. A first unbound bind/send packet arriving after all ownership records have been withdrawn still needs upstream creation authority/issuance; absence is not a replay tombstone. Broader Codex control/RPC admission, ordinary provider.message.update, receipt/notification provenance and cross-host cancellation delivery remain separate boundaries. No global retired-generation history was introduced, and already-admitted HTTP side effects may finish while retaining their original publication owner.

### Feishu first-creation replay authority — 2026-10-20

The previous control audit's remaining unbound-creation gap is now closed at the [creation authorization boundary](../src/feishu/creation.v). A first streamed send needs both a server-issued generation and its own one-use OS-random 256-bit `creation_token`; parallel authorized sends can share one generation while spending distinct tokens. First bind needs a separate, fresh creation generation and a bind-only token tied to its physical message ID. Existing-target bind keeps its captured-generation CAS contract. Missing, expired, consumed, revoked, wrong-stream, wrong-instance or wrong-operation credentials fail before HTTP or buffer/target publication. Command ingress never issues a replacement token or recovers current authority from an ID.

The [trusted application service](../src/feishu_creation_runtime.v) and [request-only Vjsx capability](../src/executor/inproc_vjsx_creation_api.v) issue credentials; `vhttpdHost.issueFeishuStream(streamId, instance, messageId?)` returns JSON containing generation/token, with an optional physical target for bind-only issuance. The [bridge endpoint](../src/feishu_card_bridge_server_runtime.v) issues through the existing authenticated peer channel and independently spends its own grants before send. Local remote-issuance reservations are captured before proxy I/O; withdrawal, replacement or expiry removes their exact owner, so late responses cannot reinstall authority. Completion exchanges one reserved capacity slot for one grant and succeeds once. Outstanding grants and reservations together are capped at 4096 per state, expire after 300 seconds, and are removed on consumption/revocation; capacity fails closed without evicting live grants. No retired-generation history is retained.

[Physical message mutation](../src/feishu/initial_send.v) now also invalidates outstanding first-bind grants and remote bind issuances for that ID, even across streams. A grant issued before another stream rebinds and withdraws the same message cannot later recreate it. [Qualified clear](../src/feishu/control_generation.v) can cancel a grant before the first buffer/HTTP admission, including a target-specific clear for an as-yet unbound message. Target-specific cancellation preserves other physical-target grants and generations. Last-owner retirement removes otherwise unspent same-generation tickets; [failure admission and final deletion](../src/feishu/stream_command.v) apply the same lifetime rule while preserving already-admitted peers. Trusted clear/rebind paths retire old/new stream creation ownership. Selection/publication retain the existing FeishuState -> CodexState lock order, and issuance transport/notification callbacks stay outside it.

The [TypeScript producer](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/state.mjs) obtains credentials for new/derived streams and persists `stream_creation_token` with the server generation, including SQLite migration. Its [command builders](../examples/codexbot-app-ts/lib/commands.mts) serialize captured credentials; bind creation authority is separate from replacement expectation. External/PHP producers also need a trusted issuance adapter before sending first-creation packets; the previous empty/missing-token format is intentionally rejected, with no compatibility fallback. PHP sources/legacy SDK bootstrap were not changed or rerun in this slice. Streamless messages own no local buffer and retain their existing behavior. Persisted credentials do not survive expiry or hub restart as authority; renewal is an explicit trusted new issuance, not replay admission.

Final verification passes **185 distinct related V functions**: [139 integration functions](../src/feishu_creation_admission_test.v) include native/bridge replay, clear-before-delivery, duplicate concurrency, physical cross-stream replacement, existing completion/publication/rollover/finish/release paths and six actual TypeScript cancellation/derived-card/restart functions; [43 state functions](../src/feishu/creation_test.v) cover one-use/expiry/capacity, same-generation peers, physical target/mode binding, pending-target cancellation, remote-issuance retirement and terminal failure ownership; [three host lifecycle functions](../src/executor/embedded_host_test.v) verify active issuance and denial outside request lifetime. Four [Node protocol functions](../tests/protocol/feishu-stream-generation.test.mjs) pass. The final frozen-source DB-enabled OpenSSL/Boehm isolated build passes complete standard E2E: CLI 4/config-runtime 205, zero failures, standard Node/PHP/admin/provider/Relay/MCP/stream/metrics scenarios and all eight concurrent first-Relay cold-hub cases. No E2E thresholds or dependency versions changed.

Evidence is in `tmp/feishu-creation-validation/report.txt`: pre-edit originals, incremental diff, separately named intermediate diagnostics, final combined bodies/manifests, logs and hashes. All 473 staged production/support files match the workspace; final source hashes, formatting, safety lint and diff checks pass. The root binary is unchanged. Combined source hashes were verified before removing the three temporary test entries. Early interface/fixture migration and sandbox localhost-bind failures are recorded separately; intermediate passes do not replace the final source's verification. The [runtime module map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md) records the new ownership boundary; its 18 existing absolute-host-path link warnings are outside this slice, and newly added relative links resolve.

Cross-host cancellation propagation, ordinary provider.message.update, wider Codex RPC/control admission and receipt/notification provenance remain separate boundaries. Already-admitted external HTTP effects may finish while retaining their exact local publication owner. Full-project V suites, live DB service and installed WordPress opt-ins are not claimed. ### External/PHP trusted creation issuance — 2026-10-20

The [independent admin endpoint](../src/admin_feishu_creation.v) now provides authenticated discovery, issuance and revocation for external producers. It requires an explicit nonempty header token; browser sessions, query credentials and open-admin mode cannot mint worker authority. [External intent ownership](../src/feishu/external_creation.v) captures process epoch, server timestamp, random request ID and exact stream/instance/operation/physical target. Up to 4096 bounded 300-second records prevent duplicate or failed intents from minting replacement tokens. Revocation arriving before first POST also records terminal ownership. Expired, old-process or conflicting requests fail closed; consumed/revoked credentials remain unusable after replay or pruning.

The [PHP client](../php/package/src/VHttpd/Feishu/CreationClient.php) uses explicit `VHTTPD_ADMIN_BASE_URL` and `VHTTPD_ADMIN_TOKEN`, without redirects, automatic retries or renewal. [Captured leases](../php/package/src/VHttpd/Feishu/CreationLease.php) validate scope and serialize original credentials. The [PHP producer](../examples/codexbot-app/lib/Service/StreamService.php) acquires only when creating a fresh stream, saves the generation/token/original issuance request in SQLite, migrates existing tables to empty defaults, and rolls back failed task/stream writes while attempting credential revocation. Six streamed send paths pass their captured lease; cancellation clears with the saved generation. Exact remote revoke preserves peer tickets, and unpublishable late remote grants are reclaimed best-effort. This API retires unconsumed creation authority; already-admitted HTTP/live-resource cancellation retains its captured-generation contract, and general cross-host lifecycle propagation remains separate.

Verification passes **196 distinct related V functions**: 143 application/command integration functions (four new) and 53 state functions (ten new), including native and bridge replay/revoke, reordered cancellation, failed-target reservation, expiry/pruning/capacity and canonical log projection. [PHP/SQLite regressions](../php/package/tests/feishu_creation_test.php) pass, including rollback and legacy column migration. The [real PHP HTTP E2E](../tests/e2e/feishu_external_creation_test.mjs), now part of the standard suite, passes send/bind credential acquisition, header-only authority, browser/query rejection, duplicate requests, revocation, restart rejection and credential absence from terminal/event logs. Its first run exposed rejected query tokens in generic access-log paths; the creation endpoints now record only canonical paths, with the original failure retained. A direct state command initially used an invalid QuickJS path; final passing runs use the project Makefile configuration. No assertion or timeout was weakened.

The final isolated DB/OpenSSL build passes; all 472 production/support files match staging and the root binary remains unchanged. Complete standard E2E passes **4 CLI + 205 configuration/runtime checks, zero failures**, plus all standard Node/Python/PHP/admin/provider/Relay/MCP/stream scenarios and the new real PHP HTTP issuance test; all eight concurrent first-Relay cold-hub cases pass. Evidence is recorded in `tmp/feishu-external-creation-validation/report.txt`. V formatting, PHP lint, shell/Node syntax, safety lint and diff checks pass. The existing full PHP Pest suite cannot bootstrap because `VHttpd\\Upstream\\WebSocket\\EventHandler` is absent; no substitute SDK was fabricated. ### Cross-host bridge revocation propagation — 2026-10-20

[Connection authority](../src/feishu/bridge_authority.v) now owns bridge-issued grants, local mirrors, admitted initial sends and published buffers independently of a reusable client ID. Disconnect/replacement removes exact owned resources and paired Codex targets; old callbacks cannot acquire a new connection's authority. Native trusted registration detaches old bridge ownership. Wire first-bind/rebind instead retains its captured connection and original remote stream/instance/generation, so disconnect still cleans it and later clear revokes the original remote scope.

[Session clear](../src/command_handlers.v) retires local state atomically, unlocks, then invokes the optional narrow bridge revocation capability. [Proxy transport](../src/feishu_card_bridge_runtime.v) binds requests and revocation attempts to the original sender. Failed or ambiguous proxy operations close that transport and retire its local connection resources; retries of an old revoke cannot close or clear a replacement connection. Native and external late issuance check the captured connection before installing returned grants and attempt exact remote reclamation when publication is denied. Server clear validates connection and captured generation, preserving unrelated resources.

Both bridge directions have 45-second leases, renewed by current incoming activity and the existing 15-second application heartbeat before expiry. Expired authorities cannot be renewed, consumed or published, even before the one-second watchdog physically cleans them. Client watchdogs are joined provider-session workers; server watchdogs are joined by their accepted session. Thus one-way lost pongs do not leave local credentials live merely because writes succeed, and a silent peer loses remote authority. This is bounded fail-closed termination, not immediate remote confirmation during a partition; admitted external HTTP effects cannot be undone. Connection retirement also affects its other streams, which require fresh explicit issuance.

Server create intents retain at most 4096 results/failures for the connection lifetime; replay after consumption, revoke or grant expiry never remints. Full capacity rejects without evicting evidence; the native proxy closes and reconnects on failure. Retirement clears this bounded connection history. Canonical bridge access logs omit credential query strings while authentication still parses the original URL. General remote bind/rebind publication is not added; this slice preserves original remote revocation ownership.

Final verification passes **230 distinct V functions**: 63 state functions (ten new connection/lease/bind-origin cases) and 167 application/command/bridge integration functions (four new). The isolated DB/OpenSSL/Boehm build passes, with all 473 production/support hashes matching staging and the root binary unchanged. Complete standard E2E passes **4 CLI + 205 configuration/runtime checks, zero failures**, plus Node/Python/PHP/admin/provider/Relay/MCP/stream scenarios and all eight concurrent first-Relay cold-hub cases. The new actual two-hub case passes remote replacement, late HTTP, failed revoke/reconnect, old retry isolation and real server lease expiry; its TCP relay drops worker-bound replies to verify client expiry and reconnection too (about 60 seconds). Formatting, syntax, safety lint and diff checks pass. Initial fixture pointer/client-ID issues, a sampled fixture wait, metadata alias compilation and a canonical-path/auth query regression are preserved separately; assertion strengths and existing deadlines were not relaxed. Evidence is in `tmp/feishu-cross-host-revocation-validation/report.txt`.

Root binary replacement, commits/PRs, whole-project V suites, live DB-service and installed WordPress opt-ins are not part of this validation. The [runtime module map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md) records the new owners and lifetime contract.

## Milestone 1 — Replace `unsafe { voidptr(&app) }` bridge (2–3 days)

**Goal:** delete `executor_bridge.v` and remove the V-codegen-bug workaround.

**Scope:** the entire `executor.AppFacade` interface in `src/executor/`.

**Plan:**

1. Read every `AppFacade` method in `src/executor/app_facade*.v`. Catalog the fields of `App` it transitively reads.
2. Replace each method's signature with concrete-value arguments (e.g. `runtime_config_json string`, `plan_json string`, `read_timeout_ms_for_kind fn(string) int`).
3. Move the call sites that build the `AppFacade` to pass those values directly. Most call sites live in `src/app_runtime_builder.v` and `src/executor/`.
4. Delete `src/executor_bridge.v` and its 194 lines of `unsafe { &App(w.app_ptr) }` blocks.
5. Add a `grep -r "voidptr(&app)\|unsafe { &App" src/` check in `scripts/lint_safety.sh` so the pattern cannot reappear unnoticed.

**Files touched (estimate):** `src/executor/app_facade*.v`, `src/app_runtime_builder.v`, `src/executor_bridge.v` (delete), `scripts/lint_safety.sh` (new).

**Verification:**

- `make prod` succeeds on linux-amd64 + macos-arm64.
- `make test-fast` passes with no new failures.
- `grep -r "unsafe { &App\|voidptr(&app)" src/` returns nothing.
- `worker_backend_dispatch_*` integration tests pass (they go through the AppFacade).

**Rollback:** revert the merge commit. The bridge is isolated to one file so the revert should be clean.

**Risk:** HIGH (touches the interface used by hot paths). Mitigation: keep the change behind a single feature branch; merge only after `test-all` passes locally and CI is green for both platforms.

---

## Milestone 2 — `App` decomposition: `ExecutorHost` + `worker_backend` (1 week)

**Goal:** break the circular dependency that blocks Phase 2.1 of `TODO_REFACTOR.md`.

**Scope:** `src/executor/`, `src/worker/`, `src/worker_backend_*.v`.

**Plan:**

1. Define `ExecutorHost` interface in `src/executor/host.v`:
   - methods that today read `app.engines.*`, `app.protocols.*`, etc.
   - this is the seam the `unsafe` bridge currently fakes.
2. Change `LogicExecutor` interface methods from `fn (mut app App)` to `fn (mut host ExecutorHost)`.
3. For `worker_backend_*.v`: introduce `WorkerState` struct holding `pool / queue / lifecycle / drain` fields. Move methods from `App` to `WorkerState` where they don't need other subsystems.
4. Add `WorkerState` as a `&mut` field on `App` (one transitional step before Milestone 3 removes it from `App` entirely).
5. Run `make test-fast` after each file move; commit green.

**Files touched (estimate):** ~15 files. `src/executor/host.v` (new), `src/worker/connection.v`, `src/worker_backend_*.v` (12 files), `src/command_*.v` (interface call sites).

**Verification:**

- `make test-fast` green.
- `grep -c "fn (mut app App)" src/worker_backend_*.v` drops by at least 50%.
- `wc -l src/main.v` should not increase.

**Rollback:** revert merge. The interface extraction is additive — old `App` methods can stay until the new path is fully wired.

**Risk:** MED. Mostly mechanical; the hard part is keeping the lock order in `src/server.v:4-24` intact.

---

## Milestone 3 — `App` decomposition: `command` and `feishu` (1 week)

**Goal:** continue Phase 2.1 modularization; bring `command_handlers.v` off `App`.

**Scope:** `src/command/`, `src/feishu/`, `src/feishu_card_bridge_*.v`.

**Plan:**

1. Define `CommandContext` and `FeishuState` structs that bundle the state `command_handlers.v` and `feishu_card_bridge_*.v` read off `App`.
2. Replace `app &App = unsafe { nil }` global in `src/command_handlers.v` with explicit injection.
3. Replace the `unsafe { nil }` placeholders in `src/feishu_card_bridge_*.v` (5+ sites) with struct fields initialized through a builder.
4. After both done: drop the `unsafe { nil }` pattern from `src/engine_runtime.v:9` (the `emit_fn` global) — give `EngineRuntime` an explicit callback field.

**Files touched (estimate):** ~12 files.

**Verification:**

- `grep -rn "unsafe { nil }" src/` shrinks by at least 8 sites.
- `make test-codexbot-fast` green (the integration tests cover feishu + command paths).
- `make test-fast` green.

**Rollback:** revert merge; the `unsafe { nil }` global pattern remains functional.

**Risk:** MED. The lock order doc in `server.v` is the safety net — keep it updated as fields move.

## Milestone 4 — Consolidate logging (½ day)

**Completed — 2026-10-22:** runtime trace 与 emit 已统一到配置事件路径；原业务事件、统计、文本/WARN 契约保留，可选 JSON stderr 和失败诊断已落地，相关回归及完整生产 E2E 通过。最终输入、二进制、失败历史与验证范围见本文顶部 M4 收尾节。下方原始提案保留作历史：未新增第二个 trace 路径，说明文档使用现有日志系统页面；验证按用户要求执行相关回归与完整生产 E2E。

**Goal:** merge `runtime_trace` (`src/main.v:33`) and `app.emit` (`src/app_composition_runtime.v:74`) into a single log path. Fixes finding 2.4 and 2.8 in one move.

**Scope:** `src/main.v`, `src/app_composition_runtime.v`, `src/logging/`.

**Plan:**

1. Add a `runtime_trace_path` config field (optional). When unset, trace events flow through `app.emit` only.
2. Delete the `runtime_trace` function in `src/main.v` and its 4 hardcoded labels. Replace each call site with `app.emit('runtime.trace', fields)`.
3. Extend `app.emit` to handle trace events (`server.started`, `admin.started`, etc.) by writing them to `control.event_log` instead of `/tmp/vhttpd_runtime_trace.log`.
4. Document the new log path in `docs/OVERVIEW.md` (fixes 2.10).
5. Add a `VHTTPD_LOG_FORMAT=json` flag (also addresses Phase 4 of `TODO_REFACTOR.md`).

**Files touched:** `src/main.v`, `src/app_composition_runtime.v`, `src/config/runtime_config.v`, `docs/OVERVIEW.md`.

**Verification:**

- `grep -n "/tmp/vhttpd_runtime_trace" src/` returns nothing.
- Boot the binary, hit a few endpoints, confirm `control.event_log` contains the same event names that `runtime_trace` used to log.
- `make test-fast` green.

**Rollback:** revert merge; the original two-path system still works.

**Risk:** LOW. The only behavioral change is where trace events land; both paths remain functional during the transition.

## Milestone 5 — CI: e2e + inproc on every PR (½ day)

**2026-10-22 实现进度：** PR 已补入 Linux amd64、macOS Intel/ARM 的 `test-inproc` 与 `test-codexbot-fast`，取消 PR 路径过滤，避免测试/PHP/CI 变更漏跑；原 fast → prod → E2E 保留，并显式使用本次构建的绝对二进制路径。新增 03:00 UTC 的 `nightly-full.yml` 与共享 `runtime-regression.yml`，三平台执行 V 全量、PHP package、锁文件安装的 CodexBot Pest、生产构建及完整 E2E，失败也上传日志。按用户确认，`test-all` 覆盖 lifecycle，不重复调用独立 lifecycle 入口。`sync-vphp-package.yml` 保持只发布 `php/package` 子树，`php/app` 有意不触发包发布；它的变更会触发运行时 PR 检查。

依赖使用固定 V/vjsx/VC/TCC/QuickJS 提交及仓库内补丁，覆盖前轮验收的 JSON 解码器、HTTP、WebSocket 与 vjsx 修改，不再依赖本地未发布改动或远端浮动分支。Action 固定完整 SHA，Node/Python/Composer 固定完整版本，PHP 固定 8.4 系列；已核实固定 setup-php 的 `parseVersion` 仅取前三个字符，不能把 `8.4.5` 配置误称为补丁版本锁。PHP 实际补丁版本与系统 apt/Homebrew 库均记录安装结果，未声明完全密封构建。本地验证已完成：actionlint 1.7.12、全部 workflow/composite shell 块的 shellcheck、YAML/触发/矩阵/失败上传检查及安全检查通过；从远端全新获取固定提交、应用补丁并构建 V 成功，8,227 个 V、157 个 vjsx、38 个 QuickJS 相关源码文件与前轮冻结输入无差异。用新工具链原样运行 `make test-inproc V_CC=cc WITH_DB=1` 6/6（335.33 秒）与 `make test-codexbot-fast V_CC=cc WITH_DB=1` 6/6（467.35 秒）通过；PHP package 10/10、Pest 79 项/557 断言通过。Composer 锁文件有效且兼容 PHP 8.4。Make 发现的全量为 334 个 V 文件，包含 lifecycle；本轮仅确认其覆盖和配置，没有宣称已重跑全量或生产 E2E。

证据：`tmp/m5-ci-validation/`。本地为 macOS 27.0.1 ARM、PHP 8.5.11、Node 26.12.0、Python 3.15.0、Composer 2.9.2；CI 配置为 Ubuntu 24.04/macOS 15 Intel/ARM、PHP 8.4 系列、Node 22.14.0、Python 3.12.9、Composer 2.8.8。本地通过不冒称这套远端运行时的实跑。PHP 首次在沙箱内无法监听 Unix socket，放开所需执行权限后同一入口通过；原失败保留。运行时源码/TS 夹具无漂移，原二进制保留，未继续局部审查。

**M5 配置实现与本地验证已完成，远端验收未关闭。** 完成标准：同一提交上的三个平台 fast/prod/E2E 与六个 inproc/CodexBot PR 检查通过；合并默认分支后观察 03:00 UTC 定时任务实际触发，三个 nightly 全量分支全部通过，并取得版本、补丁校验和与测试日志 artifacts。当前未推送/创建验收 PR，也没有定时运行或远端绿测证据，不核销这些验收项。Linux VM 仅只读检查，因既存跟踪修改命中技能停止规则，编译器仍为干净 `local-dev`、V 0.5.2 4f3082f；未在 VM 构建或测试。

**Goal:** catch e2e regressions on PR instead of waiting for a manual run.

**Scope:** `.github/workflows/vhttpd-binaries.yml`, `tests/e2e/run.sh`, `Makefile`.

**Plan:**

1. Add a `test-inproc-fast` job to the CI matrix that runs `make test-codexbot-fast V_CC=cc` (skip the 1103-line lifecycle test for PR, run it nightly).
2. Add a `test-e2e` job that runs `make test-e2e` after `make prod`. Use the built binary, not `vhttpd` already on PATH.
3. Add a nightly `nightly-full` workflow on `schedule: cron: '0 3 * * *'` that runs `make test-all` and `make test-codexbot-lifecycle`.
4. Update `sync-vphp-package.yml` trigger to include `php/app/**` (or document the deliberate exclusion).

**Files touched:** `.github/workflows/vhttpd-binaries.yml` (add jobs), `.github/workflows/nightly-full.yml` (new), `.github/workflows/sync-vphp-package.yml` (paths).

**Verification:**

- Open a throwaway PR; observe both new jobs run.
- Nightly job fires at 03:00 UTC and produces artifacts.
- `make test-all` exits 0 in the nightly job.

**Rollback:** disable the workflow files via `gh workflow disable`.

**Risk:** LOW. CI-only changes; no production surface affected.

## Milestone 6 — Non-blocking backoff (1 day)

**Goal:** replace `time.sleep` in request/connection paths with non-blocking backoff. Addresses finding 2.6.

**Scope:** 10 files listed in the review.

**Plan:**

1. Convert each `time.sleep(N seconds)` in `feishu_card_bridge_client_runtime.v:112` and `:161` to a 5-step backoff with a check for shutdown in between.
2. Convert `feishu_provider_connection_runtime.v:31` to `select { <-time.after(...) }` if V's stdlib supports it, otherwise a poll loop that respects a `done chan`.
3. For the small (200ms–500ms) sleeps in `codex_provider_websocket_runtime.v`, `mcp_session_stream_runtime.v`, etc.: keep `time.sleep` but add a comment that they're bounded polling and verify the total worst-case latency is acceptable.

**Files touched:** ~10 files.

**Verification:**

- `make test-codexbot-fast` green.
- Manual: open a feishu card bridge, kill the upstream, observe the reconnect loop without freezing the websocket send path for > 1s at a time.

**Rollback:** revert merge; the time.sleep calls revert.

**Risk:** LOW. Behavior change is bounded.

2. Add a "Deprecation Timeline" section to `README.md` listing which keys are deprecated and the removal date.
3. Move `src/config/v1_plan_compat.v` and `src/config/v1_plan_compat_test.v` into `src/config/compat/v1/` so the v2 path is the primary layout.
4. Update `vhttpd.toml` to use v2 syntax; keep the file at `examples/v1-vhttpd.toml` for reference.

**Files touched:** `src/config/v1_plan_compat.v`, `vhttpd.toml`, `README.md`, directory moves.

**Verification:**

- README deprecation section renders correctly in `mkdocs serve`.

**Rollback:** revert merge.

**Risk:** LOW. Pure documentation + file moves; runtime unchanged.

---

## Sequencing

| Order | Milestone | Depends on | Owner signal |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Preparation | — | baseline docs in `docs/perf-baseline.md` |
| 1 | Drop unsafe bridge | M0 | no `unsafe { &App(` in src |
| 2 | ExecutorHost + worker_backend | M1 | `grep -c "fn (mut app App)" src/worker_backend_*.v` ↓ 50% |
| 3 | command + feishu decomposition | M1, M2 | `grep -rn "unsafe { nil }" src/` ↓ 8 sites |
| 4 | Logging consolidation | — | no `/tmp/vhttpd_runtime_trace.log` |
| 5 | CI e2e + inproc | — | new jobs in CI matrix |
| 6 | Non-blocking backoff | — | no `time.sleep` ≥ 1s in hot paths |
| 7 | v1 deprecation | — | `@[deprecated]` markers + README section |

**Independent tracks:** M4, M5, M6, M7 can run in parallel. M1 → M2 → M3 must be sequential because they each remove a layer of `App` coupling.

**Target end state:**

- `App` struct holds only `DataPlaneRuntime` + `ControlPlaneRuntime` + `veb` boilerplate; sub-runtimes are separate structs.
- No `unsafe { &App(` in production code.
- No `unsafe { nil }` in `src/command_handlers.v` or `src/admin_server.v`.
- No hardcoded `/tmp` paths.
- One log path through `app.emit`.
- v1 deprecation public; v2 is the default config example.

---

### 跨宿主绑定发布边界（2026-10-21）

已增加 bind/rebind 的本地待确认预留、远端源代际 CAS 与确认后的配对发布；撤销、连接替换及迟到结果通过身份检查和原连接上的精确补偿收敛。协议、状态归属及分布式确认限制见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。相关状态、命令与真实桥接回归纳入现有测试入口；完整验证证据保存在本轮工作区的 `tmp/feishu-bridge-bind-validation/`。

### 跨宿主流式操作完成边界（2026-10-21）

append/finish/fail 已增加原连接及代际确认、逐镜像排队、完成时身份复核和迟到补偿；finish 同步封口，fail 配对清理缓冲链与 Codex 目标。发起端镜像禁止写回 provider，PHP 更新回执也不再发布绑定。协议、升级及分布式确认限制见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)；本轮相关回归和标准 E2E 的证据保存在 `tmp/feishu-bridge-stream-validation/`。

### 生产端 sent 回执绑定发布边界（2026-10-21）

原始 PHP 实现可复现迟到 `feishu.message.sent` 覆盖新代际及改写已取消绑定。创建服务现于返回命令前持久化发布 ID 和实例，可信创建请求 ID 随命令传递；原生及桥接发布在同一状态锁内捕获不可变回执，绑定独立计数、分段链共享递增序号。生产端以一条 SQLite UPDATE 匹配预存发布 ID、实例、有效任务/流状态、递增序号及确认后的代际；显式重绑先 CAS 换用新发布 ID，终态撤回旧权限。缺字段、旧格式、旧发布、乱序或重复回执不会补发凭证、创建记录或恢复旧绑定。协议与升级限制见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

最终相关回归通过 259 个 V 函数（81 状态层、178 集成层，6 个新增），包含实际 V 回执 JSON 到真实 PHP 路由/SQLite 的互操作；独立 PHP 创建、回执、双连接 SQLite、撤销、删除及迁移检查通过。完整标准 E2E 通过 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查，零失败，并通过新增真实 hub/Unix worker/PHP 回执乱序用例、双 hub 撤销/重连及全部 8 次首条 Relay 并发冷启动。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建通过，480 个生产输入与工作区一致，根目录二进制未替换。最终一次测试夹具 vfmt 仅展开返回结构体；其编译输入的规范格式与最终源文件完全一致。

证据与逐文件原始快照保存在 `tmp/feishu-sent-publication-validation/report.txt`。完整旧 PHP Pest 仍因缺少 `VHttpd\Upstream\WebSocket\EventHandler` 无法启动，未伪造 SDK；Linux 按 VM 技能的已有跟踪变更停止规则仅做只读检查，无构建、测试或环境修改。普通 provider update、任意外部事件来源认证、生产端之外的撤销订阅、全项目 V 和长期负载仍不属于本轮。生产者需先撤销/替换自己的预留，本轮不撤回已完成的 HTTP 副作用。

### 普通 provider.message.update 边界（2026-10-21）

普通 Feishu update 现匹配既有绑定的 scope、实例、消息 ID、非空代际及原桥接连接；准入捕获独立操作身份和内容快照，排队醒来后复核，异步完成只提交原生命周期。整卡成功保留绑定并关闭旧流式发布权，重绑恢复新的流式生命周期；桥接确认须携带原消息、代际和 message 状态，不确定确认退休原连接。模板使用所有端准入时内容且只替换一次，客户端快照不能覆盖所有端内容。PHP/TS 更新生产者传递保存的绑定上下文；通用入口拒绝缺 provider 更新和无替换语义的 Codex update fallback。协议与外部 HTTP 副作用限制见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

相关验证通过 89 个状态回归、187 个集成回归及 79 个生产端回归（其中 3 个生产端用例也纳入集成组）；PHP/SQLite 和 Node 协议测试通过。真实 App 执行器的前后对照确认空 provider 绕过路径已封堵。完整标准 E2E 通过全部测试，包括 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查、新增普通更新 HTTP 在途重绑/撤销/同 ID 重建及模板所有权用例、双 hub、sent 回执和冷启动 Relay。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建通过，481 个输入与源码一致，根目录二进制未替换；格式、安全检查和本轮文件差异检查通过。

原桥接测试夹具改为读取实际发出的完整 WebSocket 帧后再消费等待者，消除夹具抢先撤回发送租约的竞态。模板 E2E 初次预期遗漏已有 initial 内容，已根据实际 HTTP payload 修正为解析 JSON 后精确比较；不改变生产语义。临时 sent 通知 fallback 探针因该入口不执行返回命令而无效，已撤回，不作为覆盖证据。完整证据、原始快照、补丁、输入哈希及诊断在 `tmp/feishu-message-update-validation/report.txt`。

完整旧 PHP Pest 仍因缺少 `VHttpd\Upstream\WebSocket\EventHandler` 无法启动。Linux 按 VM 测试技能的既存跟踪变更停止规则仅做只读预检及最终状态检查，无构建、测试或环境修改。已准入外部 PATCH 不能撤回；本轮保护本地完成发布和新代际资源，未宣称覆盖外部来源认证、全项目 V 或长期负载。

### 原始 provider.message.send 边界（2026-10-21）

旧版真实 HTTP 与状态测试均复现：删去 stream 后原始 Feishu send 可跳过可信创建凭证。现 wire send 必须携带非空 scope 和一次性创建凭证，通用入口拒绝缺 provider 及 Codex 普通 send fallback；Feishu 专用桥接拒绝转投其他 provider，并在消耗凭证前完成该检查。异步发布还匹配签发代际；桥接成功确认必须有非空消息 ID 和原代际，空消息 ID 的 HTTP 结果不能报告为提交成功。重绑、撤销和连接替换继续按原操作身份及物理 ID 冲突记录阻止旧资源发布。协议、可信 admin/宿主入口与外部 POST 副作用限制见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

本轮兼容性收紧包括旧的一次性无 scope 回复：调用方需先显式取得可信创建上下文，不在命令准入时补签或刷新旧代际；Codex 原始 send 改用 RPC/控制入口，桥接两端需同步升级。

最终 281 个相关 V 回归通过（91 状态、190 集成），覆盖真实执行器路由、无 scope 重放、异常完成确认、重绑/撤销/断连与旧清理。完整标准 E2E 通过所有套件，包括 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查、新增 provider 转投拒绝且不消耗凭证、空 HTTP 消息 ID、旧 POST 在途时替换连接并重建同一消息、双 hub、sent 和冷启动 Relay。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建通过，481 个输入匹配源码，根目录二进制未替换；格式、安全和本轮差异检查通过。

测试夹具补齐真实 Feishu 路由注册及 narrow-host 成功路径的签发凭证；新增 E2E 清理改用重连后的当前连接和代际。V 对断言内条件表达式生成未声明 C 临时变量，改为等价的预期值局部变量后通过，未更改编译器或删除断言。精确输入、原始快照、补丁、修复前复现与完整日志见 `tmp/feishu-raw-send-validation/report.txt`。Linux 因既存跟踪变更按 VM 技能停止规则仅做只读预检及最终检查，无构建、测试或环境修改；未宣称全项目 V 或长期负载覆盖。

### PHP/TS 一次性回复可信创建上下文（2026-10-21）

CodexBot 的 PHP/TS 一次性文本、帮助卡片和恢复提示已迁移到显式可信签发：每条回复捕获独立 scope、实例、代际和凭证，序列化输出 send 与同代际 clear；撤销后重放不重新签发，签发失败不退回无 scope 命令。PHP 依赖图注入既有 CreationClient，事件与 TS frame 按调用捕获实例；长任务和审批继续使用已保存的上下文。协议与归属说明见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

真实 PHP HTTP 签发 E2E 另复现 control/data 分配独立 App，导致合法 admin 凭证无法消费。现仅当 control 无业务 pipeline 且存在唯一可用的非 WebSocket 业务 App 时共享该 App，统一凭证、撤销和关闭归属；缺少或存在多个候选时不合并。修复前原生探针及修复后互操作日志均已保留。

完整标准 E2E 已通过，包括 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查、新增真实 PHP/TS 获取、消费、精确清理、撤销及重放；独立 PHP/Node 协议、实例隔离、App 生命周期和 server logic 回归通过。真实 TS 生产端 80 个函数全部通过，加上实例隔离 1、归属查询 4、App 生命周期 7 和 server logic 81，本轮共通过 173 个相关 V 回归函数；精确构建输入和完整证据记录在 `tmp/feishu-one-shot-validation/report.txt`。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建通过，481 个输入匹配源码，根目录二进制未替换。

旧 PHP Pest 仍在启动前因缺少 `VHttpd\Upstream\WebSocket\EventHandler` 失败，未伪造 SDK；Linux 因既存跟踪变更按 VM 技能停止规则仅做只读检查，未构建、测试或修改环境。未送达的临时凭证由服务端过期回收；已准入外部 HTTP 副作用不能撤回，本轮不宣称入站业务事件幂等或来源认证覆盖。

### 一次性回复失败与清理边界（2026-10-21）

上一版真实 E2E 可复现：省略 clear 后，一次性回复仍接受 append，说明发送与清理之间的中断会留下流缓冲。原生与桥接现在将保留的 `feishu:reply:` scope 作为终结式发送：先消费一次性凭证，完成时仍校验原 scope、代际、物理消息冲突和连接身份，但不发布缓冲、Codex 目标或 sent 绑定。后续 clear 不再承担成功发送的资源回收；失败发送与回执投递中断继续通过原 admission 的 defer 释放。详见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

PHP 签发响应丢失、HTTP/JSON/凭证校验失败时，撤销捕获的原始请求身份，不补签、不重试签发、不以清理失败掩盖原错误；PHP/TS 的不可序列化内容在签发前拒绝。未送达且撤销不可达的凭证仍按原始 300 秒期限与容量限制约束，不承诺网络故障时立即撤销；已经消费的凭证不会因发送失败恢复有效。

最终源码通过 140 个相关 V 回归函数（96 状态、44 集成，含 7 个新增），PHP 包与协议检查、Node 3 项检查均通过。完整标准 E2E 在最终隔离二进制上通过所有套件，包括 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查、新增真实 PHP/TS 的 provider 拒绝、网络断开、遗漏/无效 clear、签发响应损坏后按原请求撤销与重放拒绝。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 构建通过，481 个生产输入匹配源码；格式、安全、语法和本轮差异检查通过。精确输入、前后对照和结果记录在 `tmp/feishu-reply-failure-validation/report.txt`。旧 PHP Pest 仍因缺少 SDK 接口无法启动；Linux 因既存跟踪变更按 VM 技能停止规则仅做只读检查。根目录二进制未替换；已准入的外部 HTTP 副作用不可撤回。

### 未消费创建凭证的过期与容量回收（2026-10-21）

修复前状态回归复现：过期的首次 bind 凭证在惰性回收前仍可授权 clear，并撤销同目标的新凭证；发现接口、已过期请求和过期 finish 也不能共同回收三类遗留记录。现在 clear 先清除过期权限，grant、签发预留和外部请求 memo 共用回收逻辑；可信发现、签发、外部请求校验、finish 和既有 provider 维护入口均可回收，不新增后台任务，不撤回已经准入的 HTTP 操作。详见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

未过期的失败/撤销证据仍保留到原请求期限，满容量拒绝新意图；不能通过提前删除 memo 恢复签发。桥接 create 去重记录仍属于物理连接，容量满后更换连接权限域，旧连接和凭证保持退休。签发失败、执行中断或撤销不可达不延长原始 300 秒有效期，过期请求与重启前 epoch 均不能恢复资源。

最终 159 个相关 V 回归通过（103 状态、56 运行时集成，含 7 个新增）；PHP 包/协议及 Node 8 项检查通过。真实 HTTP 回归使用服务端时间验证过期，实际填满 4096 个未消费意图，检查拒绝超额、不驱逐有效撤销证据及重启隔离。完整标准 E2E 通过全部套件，包括 4 CLI + 205 配置/运行时检查。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建通过，481 个生产输入匹配源码；格式、安全和本轮差异检查通过，根目录二进制未替换。原始快照、前后对照、精确输入和日志见 `tmp/feishu-creation-expiry-validation/report.txt`。

旧 PHP Pest 仍因缺少 `VHttpd\Upstream\WebSocket\EventHandler` 在启动前失败。Linux 按 VM 技能的既存跟踪变更停止规则仅做只读预检及最终检查，无构建、测试、安装或环境修改。未宣称全项目 V、长期负载或实时跨网络撤销保证。

### 桥接创建凭证满容量后的连接轮换（2026-10-21）

修复前真实代理回归复现：明确的 `bridge_creation_capacity` 拒绝仍触发通用错误断连，连带删除有效绑定。现仅对已关联且无凭证的 create 容量拒绝保留连接；矛盾 ACK、超时或不确定失败仍退休捕获的原传输。连接 memo 容量标记归该连接授权，只有 grant、缓冲、发布、签发预留、待完成 RPC 和回调回执均结束才原子摘除并重连；全局 grant 容量拒绝不轮换，不重试签发或驱逐活连接的重放证据。详见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

真实双 hub 测试还复现 WebSocket 读循环同步执行命令、等待同一读循环处理代理结果的超时。回调改由每连接容量 16 的串行会话队列执行，读循环持续处理回执；任务捕获原 sender 和单次释放租约，回执交付后再允许空闲轮换。停止时取消原连接等待、排空任务并等待 worker；旧任务和旧清理不转投新连接或新一代缓冲。

最终 147 个相关 V 回归已通过（107 状态、40 集成，含 7 个新增）；新增双 hub E2E 验证有效绑定保留、clear 回执先于轮换、同消息 ID 重建及迟到签发隔离。双 hub 在真实传输中注入签发前容量 ACK，模块回归检查实际 memo 上限。PHP 测试目标、DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建、格式、安全、语法及本轮差异检查通过；481 个暂存文件匹配源码，根目录二进制未替换。完整标准 E2E 全部通过，包括新增容量轮换、双 hub、真实 PHP/TS、Relay、4 项 CLI 和 205 项配置/运行时检查；完整证据见 `tmp/feishu-bridge-capacity-validation/report.txt`。

Linux 因既存跟踪变更按 VM 技能停止规则只做只读预检和最终检查，无构建、测试、安装或环境修改。真实网络故障仍退休其资源；不能撤回已准入的外部 HTTP 副作用，未宣称全项目 V 或长期负载覆盖。

### 桥接回调队列背压与停止（2026-10-21）

原始生产快照的真实 TCP 背压回归已复现：大帧写出受阻时，满队列的同步 503 回执挡住 WebSocket 读循环。现任务和拒绝回执分别使用容量 16 的会话队列；读循环仅做非阻塞准入，拒绝队列也满或拒绝写出失败时终止捕获的原会话。回执持有原 dispatch 租约直到写出或失败，避免空闲轮换提前截断回复。详见 [Runtime Module Map](RUNTIME_MODULE_MAP.md)。

两个队列共享关闭准入门锁，关闭后迟到入队只能释放原租约，不能落到已排空通道。watchdog 因原注册归属消失退出时也停止原 session，打断原 socket IO、取消 RPC 等待并排空队列；所有 worker 继续由会话等待退出。旧 abort、清理和迟到回执只使用原 sender，不能删除替换后的连接或缓冲。

最终 45 个相关 V 回归全部通过（含 5 个新增），原始快照在目标读循环阻塞断言处失败。最终双 hub E2E 已验证等待中的 RPC、16 个排队任务、4 个 503 拒绝、恢复后的完成回执、断连及新代资源隔离，以及带积压 SIGTERM 在 4.5 秒上限内退出。DB/OpenSSL/QuickJS/Boehm 隔离构建、格式、安全、语法和本轮差异检查通过，481 个暂存文件匹配源码；根目录二进制保持原样。完整标准 E2E 全部通过，包括新增回调队列、桥接、PHP/TS、Relay、4 项 CLI 与 205 项配置/运行时检查。最终结果、原始快照、补丁和精确输入见 `tmp/feishu-bridge-queue-validation/report.txt`。

Linux 因既存跟踪变更按 VM 技能停止规则仅做只读检查，无构建、测试、安装或环境修改。已经执行业务的回调沿用执行器超时，不声称能撤回已准入的外部副作用，也未宣称全项目 V 或长期负载覆盖。
